(`USC00444909`) yields a 100-year Air Freezing Index ($AFI_{100yr}$) of **556 degree F-days** ($> 500$ F-days). The superseded 1951\u20131980 product (416 F-days) was set aside per Loudoun County AHJ Amendment 3.\n   - Mandated frost depth is **42 inches** (3.50 ft). A uniform bearing depth of **42 inches** was established across all 36 foundations on all 6 pads, dimensioned to the project 3-inch grid and satisfying UFC 3-220-01 $\\ge 18$ in and Basis of Design $\\le 180$ in.\n\n2. **Bearing Stratum & Groundwater**:\n   - Contact between Stratum I (residuum) and Stratum II (decomposed rock) ranges between 4.5 ft and 14.0 ft below finished grade across the campus. At 3.5 ft depth, all 36 foundations bear in competent **Stratum I** residuum ($\\gamma = 118$ pcf, $\\phi = 26.0^\\circ$, $c = 250$ psf).\n   - 30-day stabilised groundwater depths range from 8.0 ft to 21.0 ft. Because footing excavations terminate at 3.5 ft, no excavation penetrates the water table ($\\text{dewatering days} = 0$, dewatering cost = $0). Groundwater unit weight corrections were incorporated into bearing capacity analyses per EM 1110-1-1905.\n\n3. **Bearing Capacity & Settlement Performance**:\n   - Presumptive bearing values were strictly prohibited per AHJ Amendment 4 and UFC 3-220-01. Site-specific capacities were evaluated using three accepted methods (Hansen, Meyerhof, Vesic), with Hansen governing.\n   - Factors of safety on bearing capacity conform to UFC 3-220-01 Table 2-3: **3.5** for Light Industrial (`CR-P1`, `CR-P2`, `CR-P3`, `CR-P6`), **3.0** for Office (`CR-P4`), and **2.5** for Warehouse (`CR-P5`).\n   - All 36 foundations satisfy $q_{net} \\le q_{allow,net}$ and post-construction settlement $s_{tot} \\le 1.0\\text{ in}$ (maximum observed is 0.995 in on `CR-P3-F3`). Angular distortion across all thirty adjacent spans along Column Line A satisfies $\\delta / L_{span} \\le 1/500$ (0.0020).\n\n4. **Continuous Shear-Wall Footing (`W1`) Stability**:\n   - 24.0-ft long continuous footings evaluated under ASD load combinations:\n     - **Sliding ($0.6D + W$)**: $FS_{sliding} = 2.62$ to $3.03 \\ge 1.50$ (resisted by base friction $\\delta = \\frac{2}{3}\\phi = 17.33^\\circ$ without passive credit).\n     - **Overturning ($D + L + W$)**: Resultant eccentricity $e = 4.70\\text{ to }5.82\\text{ in} \\le L/6 = 48.0\\text{ in}$ (well within middle-third kern).\n     - **Net Uplift ($0.6D + W$)**: $FS_{uplift} = 2.80$ to $3.11 \\ge 1.00$.\n\n5. **Structural Concrete & Durability**:\n   - Footing thicknesses (21 to 42 in, multiples of 3 in) satisfy two-way punching shear and one-way beam shear under $1.2D + 1.6L$ ($d = t - 4\\text{ in}$).\n   - Main reinforcement satisfies factored moment and minimum ratio $\\rho \\ge 0.0018$ each way (#5 and #6 bars @ 4.2 to 5.7 in spacing $\\le 12.0\\text{ in}$).\n   - Stratum I soluble soil sulfates govern concrete durability per EM 1110-2-2000 and UFGS 03 30 00:\n     - `CR-P1`: Class S1 $\\rightarrow f'_c = 4000$ psi, $\\max w/cm = 0.50$\n     - `CR-P2`: Class S2 $\\rightarrow f'_c = 4500$ psi, $\\max w/cm = 0.45$\n     - `CR-P3`: Class S0 $\\rightarrow f'_c = 4000$ psi, $\\max w/cm = 0.50$\n     - `CR-P4`: Class S1 $\\rightarrow f'_c = 4000$ psi, $\\max w/cm = 0.50$\n     - `CR-P5`: Class S0 $\\rightarrow f'_c = 5000$ psi, $\\max w/cm = 0.40$\n     - `CR-P6`: Class S2 $\\rightarrow f'_c = 4500$ psi, $\\max w/cm = 0.45$\n\n6. **Campus Foundation Programme & Cost Optimization**:\n   - **`CR-P1`**: `IMPROVED_SPREAD` (Spread is physically infeasible; $s > 1.0$ in and column bays overlap; Improved cost: $76,736.40 vs Spread cost: $132,450.00). Governed by `BEARING`.\n   - **`CR-P2`**: `IMPROVED_SPREAD` (Heavy loads and soft residuum $E_s = 85$ ksf make spread infeasible; 11-ft piers reach Stratum II, yielding $s \\le 0.75$ in; Improved cost: $86,552.05 vs Spread cost: $148,220.00). Governed by `BEARING`.\n   - **`CR-P3`**: `SPREAD` (Stiff Stratum I $E_s = 210$ ksf underlain by rock-like Stratum II at 5.5\u20137.0 ft; Spread footings compliant at $s \\le 0.995$ in; Spread cost: $30,754.11 vs Improved cost: $52,925.68; saves $22,171.57). Governed by `SETTLEMENT`.\n   - **`CR-P4`**: `IMPROVED_SPREAD` (Direct pad cost for Improved is $43,371.54 vs $58,911.10 for Spread; saves $15,539.56. Because 2 rigs are already mobilised for P1, P2, and P6, assigning P4 to the second rig incurs $0 marginal mobilisation cost). Governed by `BEARING`.\n   - **`CR-P5`**: `SPREAD` (Light warehouse loads, $FS = 2.5$, stiff Stratum I $E_s = 250$ ksf; compact spread footings cost $16,054.78 vs $35,119.14 for Improved; saves $19,064.36). Governed by `BEARING`.\n   - **`CR-P6`**: `IMPROVED_SPREAD` (Soft residuum requires spread widths up to 33.75 ft costing $196,610.46; Improved cost: $81,753.19; saves $114,857.27). Governed by `BEARING`.\n   - **Rig Mobilisation**: $\\lceil 4 \\text{ improved pads} / 2 \\text{ pads per rig} \\rceil = \\mathbf{2\\text{ rigs}}$ (`RIG-1`: `CR-P1`, `CR-P2`; `RIG-2`: `CR-P4`, `CR-P6`).\n   - **Total Campus Package Cost**: **$405,178.68** (incorporating structural concrete, reinforcement, excavation, backfill, aggregate pier drilling, geosynthetic working platforms, 4 production modulus tests, and 2 rig mobilisations).\n\nIndependent check of the deliverables against the requirement ledger:\n56 of 56 requirements verified, 41 of 41 hard requirements.\nSuperseded documents set aside: /filesystem/dc_structural__foundation_strategy__blind/project/criteria/UFC_3-220-01N_Foundation_Design_2005_CANCELLED.pdf (replaced by /filesystem/dc_structural__foundation_strategy__blind/project/criteria/UFC_3-220-01_Geotechnical_Engineering_2012_c3.pdf);
